- **Build and deploy rules** (`.kiro/steering/builds.md`, Requirements 9.3, 9.4, 9.6; design §7, §7b, §13): never run a portal deploy while a component build runs (`pgrep -af "gdk component build"`, `pgrep -af build-custom.sh` — empty at planning time; re-check immediately before every deploy and build); one LocalServer build at a time; move `edge-cv-portal/infrastructure/cdk.out` aside and run the two guard tests green before a build; deploy flag-less (**never `-c deployGroundedSamWorker=false`**, which tears down the Grounded-SAM worker — `r2`'s pre-labeling is complete (19:46 UTC), but the auto-label of any further round `…-r3` runs on it, and the separate worker fix — PR #172, `e7547824` — is deployed to it by its own workflow); device changes are verified on hardware (group 21) before they are declared done. **The build and deploy steps of groups 18–19 are written for O18 (i)** (0.14); under (ii) 19.1 is a Portal Build Fleet build of the rebased branch — **which first requires the branch pushed to `origin` (`https://github.com/awslabs/DefectDetectionApplication`)**, since the Fleet checks out `origin/<ref>` (`build_fleet.py:325`, `build_source.py:464-467`) and `spec/car-assembly-check` is local only — published at highest + 1 at the time, and 19.3 configures the Seg floor beside the Layout_Check floor; under (iii) 19–22 wait — 0.14 names each change. **In every option the device move (19.4) is a Pinned_Revision through `POST /deployments`, preceded by the coordination gate 0.15 and followed by the Installed_Version_Check (DD22).** **Two branches deploy to the one demo portal, and this spec deploys twice** (Requirement 9.6): before the rebase of 18.1, `git -C /home/ubuntu/github/jp5/main/rfdetr-seg-support merge-base --is-ancestor c066b4c8 <base>` must succeed for the chosen base (`feature/rfdetr-seg-support` at `c066b4c8`, or `origin/main` — `268dc5f5` at the first read, `e7547824` since the merge of PR #172 on 2026-11-26 04:23 UTC; **the base is read at the time and must also contain every commit a separate workflow has by then deployed to the demo Portal — `e7547824`, the Grounded-SAM worker fix, and, once merged and deployed, the auto-label time-limit change — because a deploy of this branch from an older base would revert them**, Requirement 9.6 / design §13 (1)) and `git show <base>:edge-cv-portal/infrastructure/lib/compute-stack.ts` must show the filled Seg literal with `arm64_jp5: '1.0.62'`; before each deploy of this branch, the rebased tree's `compute-stack.ts` must carry that literal (not `{}`) and the base's head must equal the one rebased onto; **after each deploy**, the deployed `WORKFLOW_RFDETR_SEG_MIN_LOCAL_SERVER_VERSIONS` on `DeploymentsHandler` and `WorkflowPackagingHandler` equals the rebased tree's literal byte for byte and names `arm64_jp5` with a version the demo lineage reaches through 19.1 (`1.0.62 ≤ <DEMO_JP5_VERSION>`), the deployed `WORKFLOW_LAYOUT_CHECK_MIN_LOCAL_SERVER_VERSIONS` equals the configured value (`{}` after 18.2; the SSM parameter's `{"arm64_jp5": "<DEMO_JP5_VERSION>"}` after 19.3), and no other Lambda carries either — the comparison is always against the tree the deploy was made from and the account's configuration, never against a value written here, and **a check that expects `arm64_jp6: '1.0.85'` is wrong by construction** (a lab-account version of an arch the demo account does not have). After 18.2 no demo-portal deploy is made from `feature/rfdetr-seg-support` or `main` alone. The explicit-version build (19.1) runs on an **aarch64 Docker host** — **this host qualifies and is the recommended one** (`uname -m` = `aarch64`, Docker 29.8.0, and it holds the rebased tree, so no push to the public remote and no transfer is needed); otherwise a server launched with `edge-cv-portal/launch-arm64-build-server.sh` in the demo account, or the lab's arm64 build server, either of which must first receive the rebased branch (pushed to `origin` — its documented setup is `git clone https://github.com/awslabs/DefectDetectionApplication`, `launch-arm64-build-server.sh:486` — or the tree transferred) and the `dda-usw2` credentials exported for the publish step — with the script **as it is** — `SKIP_PUBLISH=1 ./gdk-component-build-and-publish.sh aarch64 5` (build only; the override is irrelevant here), then, with a fresh session and after `grep -n COMPONENT_VERSION_OVERRIDE gdk-component-build-and-publish.sh` shows the `:370` path selection and the `:399-404` assignment on the rebased head, `AWS_PROFILE=dda-usw2 AWS_REGION=us-west-2 COMPONENT_VERSION_OVERRIDE=<DEMO_JP5_VERSION> SKIP_BUILD=1 ./gdk-component-build-and-publish.sh aarch64 5` (publish only; **the override matters on this run** — it is honoured only by the ECR + S3 path, the one every LocalServer takes, and without it that path publishes **highest + 1 at the time** (a moving number on the shared lineage — read from `list-component-versions`, never written here); the sign of a forgotten override is the **missing `Publishing version: <DEMO_JP5_VERSION>` line**, never a particular number); `gdk-config.json` keeps `NEXT_PATCH`, so `SKIP_BUILD=1` still finds `greengrass-build/artifacts/<name>/NEXT_PATCH/*.zip`; the Portal Build Fleet is **not** used under (i) (`portal-build.sh:411-423` has no override; it publishes highest + 1 at the time, below the floor, and would need the branch pushed). Before the second Portal deploy (19.3) the deploying shell must resolve **`us-west-2`** (`echo $AWS_REGION`, or `CDK_DEFAULT_REGION`) because `bin/app.ts` reads the SSM fallback in `CDK_DEFAULT_REGION || AWS_REGION || AWS_DEFAULT_REGION || 'us-east-1'`. The cloud test sandbox image is a separate artifact built and pushed out of band on a native x86_64 host (18.3; `test-sandbox/README.md:179-264`). GPU training on the demo account uses `ml.g4dn.xlarge`; the two training jobs are serialised unless the quota is confirmed ≥ 2.
- One naming note, not a correction: the design's Testing strategy lists the `datasets/` script tests under one module name (`tests/test_datasets_layout_scripts.py`); this plan splits the same cases into one test file per script (2.3, 2.4, 6.2, 15.1) so each group verifies alone — the **identity-gate cases of DD22** the design places in that module land in `test_datasets_layout_reference.py` (6.2, the `--calibrate --results --local-server-version` cases) and `test_datasets_compare_variants.py` (15.1); the `batch_run.py` cases (the seventh stub route `GET /system-health`) are in `test_batch_run.py` (14.2) as the design names it.

  - [ ] 0.11 **O15 — the stray piece in the frame (design review pass 5, finding 3; requirements O15; design DD18, Error handling, Carried decisions). Carried for the owner; recommended default (i), which since 06:16 UTC needs no owner edit.** *Facts*: seven images carry a fender-shaped stray piece partly in frame at the image edge, outside the car outline, recorded as data in the Triage_File (`unlabeled_stray = 1` on exactly those seven rows); two of them are `correct` rows (`616kamr4-c3889f82…`, `616kamr4-dc7bf42a…`, both `medium`, `occluder = none`). The deployed labeling policy (0.10(c)) leaves the piece unlabeled, and `piece_counts` never counts it. If the model detects it anyway, those two images come back `duplicate` → `pass = false`: a false-fail **no `min_iou` can remove**, so without a decision Requirement 4.6(b)'s stop rule would have no exit and 10.3's "false-fails expected 0" would be unattainable by construction. *Options*: **(i) — default — keep the policy; take the two rows out of the false-fail measurement in the reader:** `assembly_triage.py` classifies a row as Known_Good iff `correct` ∧ no occluder ∧ `unlabeled_stray = 0` (`STRAY_ROWS_ARE_KNOWN_GOOD = False`, 2.4), so the two rows are **Occluded_Correct** ("something in the frame the check has no class for"), reported with their verdicts and no expectation (Requirement 1.7); The Fleet publishes highest + 1, the Portal UI's only version action is Update to latest, the floors are minima, the job records carry no deployment target, and **neither the Portal nor `list-deployments` records who made a revision** — Greengrass `ListDeployments` returns no creator; `GetDeployment`'s `dda-portal:created-by` tag (`deployments.py:1909-1912`) and the `dda-portal-deployments` table's `created_by` carry the Cognito `sub` of the shared `admin` login (`d8815300-…`, the creator of `r1`/`r2` and of revisions 16–19 alike), which names the login, not the actor; only the Build Fleet's `requested_by_username` names a session, for builds — so **this spec cannot know whether the other actor intends to revise `mic-730ai` again, cannot reserve the device or the lineage, and cannot attribute a revision: the owner's confirmation is the only channel to the other actor and this gate is the only coordination mechanism**. The design protects the window **procedurally** (the Pinned_Revision 19.4 with its pre-move reads, the Installed_Version_Check before every recorded run, the `local_server_version` header every export carries and its consumers refuse) and records the platform gate that would protect it structurally — an exact-version or build-identity check in the deployment handler — as a follow-up candidate (requirements Out of scope; 23.2). *What the owner confirms*: **(1) that the other actor's (the zone3 work's) work on `mic-730ai` is finished** — the four revisions of 22:19–22:35 UTC were its last, and it will not revise the device again — **and (2) that no other actor will revise `mic-730ai`'s deployment or needs the demo `arm64JP5` lineage from the move (19.4) until the last recorded run of groups 21–22 (the verification window)**, having told the zone3 actor; and who else publishes on the lineage (the JP5 items of `dda-portal-build-jobs` read at 19.1 are the list). *The argument available to the owner*: `<DEMO_JP5_VERSION>` is a build of this branch rebased onto a head containing `c066b4c8` — a functional superset of the `1.0.3` the actor deliberately installed (a Seg-capable build of `main`) — so the move costs the other actor nothing functional; and the rollback, if one is needed, returns the device to exactly the version it ran immediately before the move (`<PREVIOUS_VERSION>`, 19.4), never to an older one. Placeholders: `<OWNER: confirmed on <date/time UTC> — by whom; the zone3 work on mic-730ai finished: yes/no; the other actor informed: yes/no>`, `<OWNER: expected window — from the move to <date>>`. *If the confirmation cannot be given* (the other actor still needs the device or the lineage in the window), the owner chooses: wait with 19.4 until it can, or accept that an unannounced revision stops the procedure at the next Installed_Version_Check (nothing from that run is recorded; the device is moved back to `<DEMO_JP5_VERSION>` by a Pinned_Revision; the notes record the version found, the time and **what the sources give — the deployment id, revision and `creationTimestamp` from `aws greengrassv2 list-deployments --target-arn … --history-filter ALL`, the `dda-portal:created-by` tag from `get-deployment` (the shared `admin` `sub`) and the JP5 items of `dda-portal-build-jobs` — never a claim about who revised**; the owner is told the coordination rule was breached; Requirement 10.7) — `<OWNER: wait | accept the stop rule>`. *This confirmation also covers the pre-move read*: if 19.4 (a)(3)–(4) read an installed version or a deployment revision that differs from the last value recorded (for the first read, the 04:25 UTC observation — `1.0.3`, revision 19), the difference is a further revision by another actor, the new revisions go into the notes, and the POST waits until the owner has re-confirmed (1) and (2) for it — the re-confirmation is recorded here with its date. The window is closed in 23.1 with the closing Installed_Version_Check and the build-jobs items at the close. Tick when the placeholders are filled. Binds 19.4, 21.x, 22.x, 23.1, 23.2.

`Triage(rows, known_good, fail, occluded_correct, problems)` with `expected_pieces(filename) = misplaced ∪ missing`, **`multiplicity_rule(filename) -> dict[class, int]`** — the row's parsed `piece_counts`, exact (the rule of Requirement 1.4; design Interfaces) — and `join(filenames) -> (matched, images_without_row, rows_without_image)`; `read_triage(path)`: columns by name, `filename` the join key (basename of a `source-ref` / a Result_Export `file`), **Known_Good iff `verdict ∈ KNOWN_GOOD_VERDICTS` and `occluder ∈ NO_OCCLUDER_VALUES` (case-insensitive, stripped) and (`STRAY_ROWS_ARE_KNOWN_GOOD` or `unlabeled_stray == 0`)**, Fail_Set iff `misplaced` or `missing` non-empty, Occluded_Correct otherwise; piece lists split on `,`/`;`/whitespace; an unrecognised `verdict`, a piece name outside `PIECE_CLASSES`, a duplicate `filename`, a `piece_counts` cell not matching the pattern (a missing class, `rear_fender=x`, a reordered or spaced cell), a non-integer or negative `unlabeled_stray` → `problems` (listed with the row's `confidence` and `notes`, never guessed); `set`, `source_path`, `confidence`, `notes` printed, never interpreted. Pixel semantics (DD3, module docstring): pixel `(x, y)` is foreground iff the number of edges with `min_x ≤ cx < max_x` whose crossing at `X = cx` satisfies `y_cross ≤ y + 0.5` is odd — the rule the sweep implements, so 5.3's oracle compares interval lists for equality; `rle_intervals(rle, width, height)` (tokens → ints, background first, sum must equal width × height, foreground runs → intervals; the `dda_manifest.rle_decode` rules without a bitmap); `interval_iou(a, b)` (two-pointer merge; 0.0 on empty union); `position_score(rle, w, h, polygon)`; `attach_detection_ids(raw_entries, built_entries)` (DD5: first unused built entry with `label == str(class_label)`, `confidence == float(confidence)`, `(x_min, y_min, x_max, y_max) == tuple(float(v) for v in bounding_box)`; `id = None` when unmatched or `built_entries is None`; a `class_label`-less raw entry compares as `"None"` against the built `""` and stays unjoined — harmless, it qualifies for no region); `check_layout(entries, frame_size, regions, *, min_iou, min_confidence)` implementing §1 steps 1–6 exactly (regions unusable → `error`; non-list entries → `warning` "no detections to check"; unknown/non-positive frame → `warning` "frame size unknown"; per-label qualifying by `str(class_label) == label and confidence ≥ min_confidence` with non-numeric confidence = 0; no/undecodable `mask_rle` → iou `None`, status `misplaced`, **`outcome = warning`** and a problem naming the id; statuses `missing`/`duplicate`/`ok`/`misplaced`; `unexpected_classes` → `warning`; **`pass = outcome == ok and every configured class ok`** — every key of `regions`, never a hard-coded three (DD2); piece `iou` = best or `None`, rounded to 4 decimals; `detections` always a list); `run_metadata(result)`. Per label: largest external contour (`cv2.findContours`, `RETR_EXTERNAL`, `CHAIN_APPROX_NONE`), `cv2.approxPolyDP` epsilon searched geometrically (×1.5 from 0.5 px) for the largest epsilon with ≤ `--max-vertices` vertices and IoU ≥ `--min-iou` (IoU via `cv2.fillPoly` raster vs the mask), normalized by the image size; writes the `expected_regions` JSON and prints `label | vertices | iou`; exit 1 naming the label on a missing label, > 1 instance of a label, or unreachable IoU — nothing written. **Calibrate, two sources, one table, the Triage_File splitting the rows:** `--calibrate (<manifest> | --results <Result_Export> --local-server-version <v>) --regions expected_regions.json --triage <csv> [--test-files <file>] [--min-confidence 0.5] [--min-iou <deployed>]`. **Identity gate (DD22; Requirements 4.6(b), 10.9) — `--local-server-version <v>` is required whenever `--results` is given** (argparse: absent with `--results` → exit 2 with the usage text; `^\d+\.\d+\.\d+$`), and **before any record is read** the export's header must carry the strings `local_server_version` and `local_server_version_end` both equal to `<v>` and `local_server_changed` false — otherwise exit 1 naming the file and both values (an absent field — an export from an older `batch_run.py` — is named as absent), nothing computed; the value comes from `verification-notes.md` through the argument (the script is offline and reads no notes, no AWS). Then per image and label `layout.position_score(rle, w, h, polygon)` on the labeled mask (manifest form) or on the `mask_rle` of the run's best qualifying instance of that label (`--results` form: `records[].instances[]` with `class_label`, `confidence ≥ --min-confidence`, highest confidence first; frame size from the record's `layout.frame`). **Known_Good rows** give the table: per label min / p5 / median / max, the `--test-files` rows separately, the synthetic margin rows — each fender against the **other** fender's region (the swapped case) and the roof against a **vertically flipped** copy of its region (an upside-down roof, flat side up; a *horizontally* mirrored half-disc is itself and would score ≈ 1.0 — pass-4 NIT 7) — and `recommended_min_iou = min(0.70, floor05(min − 0.10))` clipped to ≥ 0.30, a WARNING when the Known_Good minimum is below 0.50, the source named (`labels` | `predicted`); a Known_Good image without a qualifying instance of a label is listed (a would-be `missing` = a false-fail) and excluded from the distribution; **a Known_Good record with more than one qualifying instance of a label (`--results` form) is listed apart as "duplicate — not a calibration matter"** (O15, 0.11: an unlabeled stray piece the model detected, or a genuine extra piece — a labeling-policy outcome the owner judges; no threshold removes it) and is likewise **excluded from the distribution and the recommendation**, its best instance's scores still printed; Occluded_Correct rows are never in either listing. **Fail_Set rows** give the true-fail check at the recommended value (or `--min-iou`): `check_layout` over the image's masks must return `pass = false` and its flagged-or-missing pieces must include the row's `misplaced ∪ missing` (`Triage.expected_pieces`); every Fail_Set image that passes and every triage-named piece that comes back `ok` is listed with its scores — occluded Fail_Set images (`occluder ≠ none`) in a separate block — and in the `--results` form such a piece is the **class-from-position signal of O13** (0.8); **Occluded_Correct rows** are printed in their own block with their scores and no expectation (0 rows by `verdict`/`occluder` alone; under O15 (i) the two stray-piece rows `c3889f82`, `dc7bf42a`, which the reader classifies through `unlabeled_stray` — 0.10(a), 0.11). In the `--results` form a record with no qualifying instance of a label, without `layout.frame`, with `instances = null`, or with `detections_truncated = true` is listed under its reason and excluded from the distribution, and every record's verdict is **re-derived** with `layout.check_layout` from its `instances`, `layout.frame`, the given regions and `--min-iou` (default: the record's own `layout.min_iou`) and compared with its exported `layout.pass`/statuses. Exit 1 on any listed record (the "duplicate — not a calibration matter" listing included — it goes to the owner, never into the recommendation), any disagreement, a non-empty Fail_Set listing, a false-fail listing, a triage problem (DD18) or the identity gate. Exit codes 0/1/2; no AWS SDK.

half-disc → ≤ 64 vertices, IoU ≥ 0.98; border-touching shape; missing-label and duplicate-label failures (exit 1, no file); a non-Known_Good golden sample refused with `--triage` (exit 1, row quoted); the `--overlay` form on a synthetic overlay body with `--metadata` and with `--size`, a `detectionsTruncated` body refused, `--overlay` without a size refused; `--calibrate --triage` from a manifest **and** from a synthetic Result_Export (Data contracts shape: `instances`, `detections_truncated`): the Known_Good table and the recommendation rule, the vertically-flipped-roof row (a half-disc scores < 1.0 against its vertical flip and ≈ 1.0 against its horizontal mirror — the reason the flip is used), a Fail_Set row that passes and a triage-named piece scoring `ok` listed and exit 1, an occluded Fail_Set row in its own block, an Occluded_Correct row (`hand`, and a `correct` row with `unlabeled_stray = 1`) printed without expectation, **a Known_Good record with two qualifying instances of one class listed as "duplicate — not a calibration matter", excluded from the recommendation (the recommended value equals the one computed without that record) and exit 1**, the would-be-missing / no-frame / null-instances / truncated listings, the re-derived-verdict disagreement flag (exit 1) and the < 0.50 warning; **the identity-gate cases (DD22)** — `--results` without `--local-server-version` → exit 2 (usage); a synthetic export whose header matches `--local-server-version` → the table is computed; `local_server_version` differing → exit 1 before any record, both values and the file named, no table; `local_server_version_end` differing → exit 1; `local_server_changed: true` → exit 1; the three fields absent → exit 1 naming them as absent; the manifest form unaffected by the flag. Then the **label run** (USER, local; needs the manifest of 1.3, the Triage_File with the O15 option of 0.11 applied in the reader (2.4) and the golden sample of 0.7): capture the first Reference_Layout from the golden sample of 0.7 with `--manifest … --triage <triage>` (the script confirms the row is Known_Good); run `--calibrate <manifest> --regions … --triage <triage> --test-files <the test names of 3.3>` over all 70 lines; set `min_iou` per the rule (Requirement 4.6(a)) — the Fail_Set true-fail listing must be empty (a non-empty one means the reference, the labels or a triage row need attention before deployment; the owner decides which); record the `expected_regions` JSON, the Known_Good table, the swapped-fender and flipped-roof scores, the Fail_Set check result and the chosen value in `verification-notes.md` ("Reference layout — labels") — these values feed 13.3 and 20.1; the predicted-mask pass (4.6(b)) is 21.2 (grayscale) and 22.2 (colour), both with `--local-server-version <DEMO_JP5_VERSION>`. Verify: `cd edge-cv-portal/backend && /home/ubuntu/.dda-test-venv/bin/python -m pytest tests/test_datasets_layout_reference.py -q -p no:cacheprovider`.
    - _Requirements: 4.3, 4.4, 4.6, 10.9, 12.4_

Then: argparse (exit 2 on usage), `GET {base}/workflows/registrations/{id}` must exist and be `registered`, `--images` readable, `--folder` must exist **on this host**, be a directory, be writable and be **empty** (exit 1 naming any leftover file; a non-existent `--folder` exits 1 with a message naming the path and stating that the script must run on the device host where `/aws_dda` is mounted). Per image in sorted filename order: assert the folder is empty; copy the image in (`shutil.copy`); `POST …/trigger` → `executionId`, `captureId` (a 409 or any non-2xx → record `status: "trigger_failed"` with the body, remove the file, continue); poll `GET …/executions/{executionId}` every `--poll-s` until `status ∉ {pending, running}` **and** the folder is empty, or `--timeout-s` elapses (`status: "timeout"`, leftover file removed and recorded); then `GET …/metadata` (→ `layout.layout` or `null`), `GET …/node-status` (→ `timings_ms.<nodeId>` from `durationMs`, `null` when absent), the execution's `startedAt`/`finishedAt` (→ `timings_ms.total`), **`GET …/executions/{executionId}/overlay` (→ `instances` with `mask_rle` and `detectionsTruncated`; `maskImage`/`maskBackground` dropped)**; a failed export GET leaves that field `null` and names the call in `error`; an overlay with `instances: null` or `detectionsTruncated: true` is recorded as is. Write the Result_Export of the Data contracts (`{workflow_id, registration_id, device, started, local_server_version, local_server_version_end, local_server_changed, records: [{file, execution_id, capture_id, status, error, layout, timings_ms, instances, detections_truncated}]}`; `--workflow-id` only stamps the export; no `data_ref_basename`; the three header fields are DD22's identity record — `layout_reference.py --calibrate --results` and `compare_variants.py` refuse an export without them or with another value). Summary line: runs, passes, fails, errors; **with `--triage`** (stdlib `csv` and `re`; **the set rules of DD18 and the `piece_counts` pattern copied inline, nothing else**: the eleven columns required — a file lacking `piece_counts`/`unlabeled_stray` makes the script refuse `--triage` naming them, so the `.v1` backup is never read by accident; Known_Good iff `verdict = correct` and `occluder ∈ {"", "none"}` and `unlabeled_stray == 0`; Fail_Set iff `misplaced_pieces` or `missing_pieces` non-empty; `piece_counts` parsed with `^front_fender=(\d+);rear_fender=(\d+);roof=(\d+)$`) it adds the **false-fail list** (Known_Good records with `pass == false`), the **false-pass list** (Fail_Set records with `pass == true` — each an O13 signal, 0.8), the Occluded_Correct records with their verdicts (no expectation), **the `duplicate` list** — every record with a `duplicate` piece status, with its set and the class, marked **expected** when the row's `piece_counts` gives that class **2** (the five two-piece rows) and **O15 signal (or a model error)** otherwise (the stray-piece signal of 0.11; the only measurement of whether the model detects the unlabeled piece — the script reads `piece_counts`, never `notes`) — and the files without a triage row. No re-derivation of verdicts here (that is `layout_reference.py --calibrate --results` / `compare_variants.py`).

Then: M1 from the two `training_metadata.json` `TEST METRICS` (`test_map50`, `test_map50_95`, `test_mask_map50`, `test_mask_map50_95`); **M3 from the device verdicts split by the Triage_File** (`assembly_triage.read_triage`): false-fail rate = Known_Good records with `layout.pass == false` / |Known_Good|, false-pass rate = Fail_Set records with `layout.pass == true` / |Fail_Set| (its complement is the true-fail rate), the per-piece agreement (flagged ∪ missing ⊇ `Triage.expected_pieces`) with occluded Fail_Set images in a separate row, the Occluded_Correct records' verdicts in their own row without expectation, and every Fail_Set piece reported `ok` listed as the **O13 signal**; M4 median and p95 of `timings_ms.model`, `.gray` (grayscale only), `.layout`, `.total`; M2 per-class mask IoU of each record's best qualifying instance (`instances[]`, highest confidence) vs the labeled RLE for the test filenames (and all 70 as a secondary column), **on one pixel grid — the record's `layout.frame`**: decode the labeled RLE with `dda_manifest.rle_decode` to a boolean array at the label's `image_size`, resample by nearest-neighbour to `(frame.width, frame.height)` when the sizes differ (`cv2.resize(..., INTER_NEAREST)`, or Pillow `Image.resize(NEAREST)` when OpenCV is absent), convert back to column-major intervals (Fortran-order flatten → run boundaries) and apply `layout.interval_iou` against `layout.rle_intervals(mask_rle, frame.width, frame.height)`; a record without `layout.frame`, or whose `instances` carry no qualifying entry of the class, is "M2 not comparable" for that class, excluded from the M2 mean/min, still counted for M3; the table names the grid per variant; **every record's verdict re-derived with `layout.check_layout` from its `instances` and `layout.frame` at its variant's threshold (`--gray-min-iou` / `--color-min-iou`)** and compared with the exported `layout` section — the consistency check that cannot change M3 (a disagreement marks the row and sets exit 1); a record whose `file` has no manifest or triage match, a record with `instances = null` or `detections_truncated = true`, a missing timing, or two exports covering different file sets is marked (exit 1 on a file-set mismatch, a triage problem or a disagreement); prints one Markdown table with the raw numbers, the ranked order under the criterion of 0.5 (false-fail, then false-pass, then M1 mask mAP50-95 within 0.02 as a tie, then M4; tie → grayscale — a ranking, the decision is 22.4's) and the `--label` values. Pure, offline, no AWS SDK. Tests `edge-cv-portal/backend/tests/test_datasets_compare_variants.py` on synthetic fixtures (a synthetic triage CSV in the real vocabulary): table shape, each metric, M3's two rows with the occluded row separate and an `ok` Fail_Set piece listed, per-variant thresholds in the re-derivation, the disagreement flag, the missing-file mark, the file-set mismatch, the metric flags, the tie rule, and M2 on one grid — a record whose `layout.frame` is half the label's `image_size` gets the label resampled and its IoU equals the analytic value for a synthetic rectangle; a record without `layout.frame` is "not comparable" and excluded from the M2 mean while still counted for M3; **the identity-gate cases (DD22)**: `--local-server-version` absent → exit 2; both headers equal to it → the table; the grey export's `local_server_version` differing → exit 1 before any metric (no table file written), file and both values named; the colour export's `local_server_version_end` differing → exit 1; `local_server_changed: true` in either → exit 1; the two exports naming two different builds that both differ from `<v>` → exit 1 naming both files; an export without the three fields → exit 1 naming them as absent.
